Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any genomics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 295. The projective resolution has length at most 937 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 295 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the genomics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 937. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on genomics structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 109. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 234 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 109 + O(1/n^234). Summing over all modes and applying the genomics structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
